<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I need to register both experiments. The state machine is: PREPARING → PENDING → QUEUED → ALLOCATED → RUNNING → ANALYZING → DONE

For each experiment, I need to:
1. Create it (status = PREPARING)
2. Transition to PENDING
3. Transition to QUEUED
4. Transition to ALLOCATED
5. Transition to RUNNING (with spec)
6. Transition to ANALYZING
7. Transition to DONE (with conclusion)
8. Set metrics

Let me start with seed 0. I'll do the transitions sequentially since each depends on the previous state being valid.


I'll create both experiments at PREPARING first, then advance them through the state transitions. Since the tool validates state transitions and creates experiments if they don't exist, I can initialize both seeds in parallel before moving them forward.
```
